## Project Ideas

- Build a RAG app using one of today’s retrieval resources.
- Build a 3D landing page or portfolio section using today’s Three.js/R3F resources.
- Design a micro-interaction or scroll animation utilizing today's gsap/motion techniques.
- Build a simple agentic workflow using today's multi-agent framework releases.
